0.0;\n    T c = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results for floating-point arrays\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if (data.empty()) {\n        printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        printf(\"Name: %s\\n\", name);\n        printf(\"Elements: 0\\n\");\n        printf(\"=== END RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t N = data.size();\n    \n    // Compute statistics\n    T sum = kahan_sum(data);\n    T min_val = data[0];\n    T max_val = data[0];\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);\n        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);\n    }\n    \n    // Compute hash\n    hash h;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        h.add(val);\n    }\n    \n    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1\n    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};\n    \n    printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n    printf(\"Name: %s\\n\", name);\n    printf(\"Elements: %zu\\n\", N);\n    printf(\"Sum: %.17e\\n\", li < numRows; ++li) {\n        const size_t i = rowBegin + li;\n        double* row = &mat[li * N];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            row[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Multiply the local row block of A (numRows x N) by the full matrix B (N x N),\n// producing the local row block of C (numRows x N).\n// Cache-blocked i-k-j ordering: the innermost loop streams contiguous rows of B\n// and C, which vectorizes well and avoids strided access.\nvoid matrixMultiplyRows(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                        std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N, const size_t numRows) {\n    constexpr size_t BI = 64;\n    constexpr size_t BK = 256;\n    constexpr size_t BJ = 2048;\n\n    std::fill(C.begin(), C.begin() + numRows * N, 0.0);\n\n    for (size_t jj = 0; jj < N; jj += BJ) {\n        const size_t jEnd = std::min(N, jj + BJ);\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < N; kk += BK) {\n            const size_t kEnd = std::min(N, kk + BK);\n            for (size_t ii = 0; ii < numRows; ii += BI) {\n                const size_t iEnd = std::min(numRows, ii + BI);\n                for (size_t i = ii; i < iEnd; ++i) {\n                    const double* __restrict__ aRow = &A[i * N];\n                    double* __restrict__ cRow = &C[i * N];\n                    for (size_t k = kk; k < kEnd; ++k) {\n                        const double a = aRow[k];\n                        const double* __restrict__ bRow = &B[k * N];\n                        for (size_t j = jj; j < jEnd; ++j) {\n                            cRow[j] += a * bRow[j];\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare.\n// A is reconstructed from the deterministic generator; B and C are full matrices.\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& B, const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += getPseudoRndValue(N, i, k) * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n\n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n\n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int size = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    int exitCode = 0;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments (every rank sees the same argv)\n    for (int i = 1; A 4-row micro-kernel\n// reuses each loaded B row for four C rows. For every C element the k-sum is\n// still accumulated strictly in increasing k, so the rounding matches a plain\n// sequential dot product (see -ffp-contract=off in CMakeLists.txt).\nvoid matrixMultiplyRows(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                        std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N, const size_t numRows) {\n    constexpr size_t BI = 32;   // rows of A/C per block\n    constexpr size_t BK = 128;  // k extent per block (B block = BK x BJ doubles)\n    constexpr size_t BJ = 512;  // columns per block (BK*BJ*8 = 512 KiB, fits in L2)\n    constexpr size_t MR = 4;    // rows handled together by the micro-kernel\n\n    std::fill(C.begin(), C.begin() + numRows * N, 0.0);\n\n    for (size_t jj = 0; jj < N; jj += BJ) {\n        const size_t jEnd = std::min(N, jj + BJ);\n        const size_t jLen = jEnd - jj;\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < N; kk += BK) {\n            const size_t kEnd = std::min(N, kk + BK);\n            for (size_t ii = 0; ii < numRows; ii += BI) {\n                const size_t iEnd = std::min(numRows, ii + BI);\n                size_t i = ii;\n                for (; i + MR <= iEnd; i += MR) {\n                    const double* __restrict__ a0 = &A[(i + 0) * N];\n                    const double* __restrict__ a1 = &A[(i + 1) * N];\n                    const double* __restrict__ a2 = &A[(i + 2) * N];\n                    const double* __restrict__ a3 = &A[(i + 3) * N];\n                    double* __restrict__ c0 = &C[(i + 0) * N + jj];\n                    double* __restrict__ c1 = &C[(i + 1) * N + jj];\n                    double* __restrict__ c2 = &C[(i + 2) * N + jj];\n                    double* __restrict__ c3 = &C[(i + 3) * N + jj];\n                    for (size_t k = kk; k < kEnd; ++k) {\n                        const double* __restrict__ b = &B[k * N + jj];\n                        const double x0 = a0[k];\n                        const double x1 = a1[k];\n                        const double x2 = a2[k];\n                        const double x3 = a3[k];\n                        for (size_t j = 0; j < jLen; ++j) {\n                            const double bj = b[j];\n                            c0[j] += x0 * bj;\n                            c1[j] += x1 * bj;\n                            c2[j] += x2 * bj;\n                            c3[j] += x3 * bj;\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                for (; i < iEnd; ++i) {\n                    const double* __restrict__ aRow = &A[i * N];\n                    double* __restrict__ cRow = &C[i * N + jj];\n                    for (size_t k = kk; k < kEnd; ++k) {\n                        const double* __restrict__ b = &B[k * N + jj];\n                        const double x = aRow[k];\n                        for (size_t j = 0; j < jLen; ++j) {\n                            cRow[j] += x * b[j];\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:start]+new+s[end:]\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nc='matmul/CMakeLists.txt'\nt=open(c).read()\nt=t.replace('''# Enable optimizations\ntarget_compile_options(matmul PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)''','''# Enable optimizations. -ffp-contract=off keeps separate multiply/add rounding so\n# results are bit-identical to the sequential reference dot product.\ntarget_compile_options(matmul PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -ffp-contract=off -Wall -Wextra)''')\nopen(c,'w').write(t)\nEOF\ncmake --build /tmp/mm_build 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error\"; The tile is loaded from C, accumulated in registers strictly\n// in increasing k, then stored back, so per-element rounding matches a plain\n// sequential dot product.\ntemplate <size_t R, size_t NR>\nstatic inline void microKernel(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n                               double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N, const size_t kBegin,\n                               const size_t kEnd) {\n    double acc[R][NR];\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r)\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < NR; ++j) acc[r][j] = C[r * N + j];\n    for (size_t k = kBegin; k < kEnd; ++k) {\n        const double* __restrict__ b = &B[k * N];\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n            const double a = A[r * N + k];\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < NR; ++j) acc[r][j] += a * b[j];\n        }\n    }\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r)\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < NR; ++j) C[r * N + j] = acc[r][j];\n}\n\n// Generic remainder: R rows x arbitrary (small) column count.\ntemplate <size_t R>\nstatic inline void microKernelTail(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n                                   double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N, const size_t jLen,\n                                   const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {\n    for (size_t k = kBegin; k < kEnd; ++k) {\n        const double* __restrict__ b = &B[k * N];\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n            const double a = A[r * N + k];\n            double* __restrict__ c = &C[r * N];\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < jLen; ++j) c[j] += a * b[j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <size_t R>\nstatic inline void rowPanel(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n                            double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N, const size_t jBegin,\n                            const size_t jEnd, const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {\n    constexpr size_t NR = 8;\n    size_t j = jBegin;\n    for (; j + NR <= jEnd; j += NR)\n        microKernel<R, NR>(A, B + j, C + j, N, kBegin, kEnd);\n    if (j < jEnd) microKernelTail<R>(A, B + j, C + j, N, jEnd - j, kBegin, kEnd);\n}\n\n// Multiply the local row block of A (numRows x N) by the full matrix B (N x N),\n// producing the local row block of C (numRows x N).\n//\n// Cache blocking over (j, k, i) with a register-tiled 4x8 micro-kernel. For\n// every C element the k-sum is accumulated strictly in increasing k, so the\n// rounding matches the sequential reference (see -ffp-contract=off in\n// CMakeLists.txt).\nvoid matrixMultiplyRows(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                        std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N, const size_t numRows) {\n    constexpr size_t BK = BKV;  // k extent per block\n    constexpr size_t BJ = BJV;  // columns per block (B block = BK x BJ doubles)\n    constexpr size_t MR = 4;    // rows per micro-kernel tile\n\n    std::fill(C.begin(), C.begin() + numRows * N, 0.0);\n\n    const double* a = A.data();\n    const double* b = B.data();\n    double* c = C.data();\n\n    for (size_t jj = 0; jj < N; jj += BJ) {\n        const size_t jEnd = std::min(N, jj + BJ);\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < N; kk += BK) {\n            const size_t kEnd = std::min(N, kk + BK);\n            size_t i = 0;\n            for (; i + MR <= numRows; i += MR)\n                rowPanel<MR>(a + i * N, b, c + i * N, N, jj, jEnd, kk, kEnd);\n            switch (numRows - i) {\n                case 3: rowPanel<3>(a + i * N, b, c + i * N, N, jj, jEnd, kk, kEnd); break;\n                case 2: rowPanel<2>(a + i * N, b, c + i * N, N, jj, jEnd, kk, kEnd); break;\n                case 1: rowPanel<1>(a + i * N, b, c + i * N, N, jj, jEnd, kk, kEnd); break;\n                default: break;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:start]+new+s[end:]\nopen('k2.cpp','w').write(s)\nEOF\nrun(){ mpicxx -O3 -march=native -ffp-contract=off -std=c++20 -DBKV=$1 -DBJV=$2 k2.cpp -o kv2 2>&1 | grep -E \"error\" ; A 4x8 tile of C is kept in vector registers\n// over a whole k block. For every C element the k-sum is accumulated strictly\n// in increasing k, so the rounding is identical to a plain sequential dot\n// product (see -ffp-contract=off in CMakeLists.txt).\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ntypedef double v4d __attribute__((vector_size(32)));\n\nconstexpr size_t NR = 8;    // tile width (one cache line of doubles)\nconstexpr size_t MR = 4;    // tile height\nconstexpr size_t BK = 256;  // k extent of a block\nconstexpr size_t BJ = 512;  // column extent of a block (packed B block = 1 MiB)\n\nstatic inline v4d loadv(const double* p) {\n    v4d v;\n    __builtin_memcpy(&v, p, sizeof(v));\n    return v;\n}\nstatic inline void storev(double* p, const v4d v) { __builtin_memcpy(p, &v, sizeof(v)); }\nstatic inline v4d bcast(const double x) { return v4d{x, x, x, x}; }\n\n// Pack B[kBegin:kEnd, jBegin:jEnd) into panels of NR columns:\n//   packed[(panel * kLen + k) * NR + c] = B[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + panel*NR + c]\n// The last panel is zero-padded when the column count is not a multiple of NR.\nstatic void packB(const double* __restrict__ B, double* __restrict__ packed, const size_t N,\n                  const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd, const size_t jBegin, const size_t jEnd) {\n    const size_t kLen = kEnd - kBegin;\n    const size_t jLen = jEnd - jBegin;\n    const size_t fullPanels = jLen / NR;\n    for (size_t p = 0; p < fullPanels; ++p) {\n        double* dst = packed + p * kLen * NR;\n        const double* src = B + kBegin * N + jBegin + p * NR;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kLen; ++k) {\n            storev(dst + k * NR, loadv(src + k * N));\n            storev(dst + k * NR + 4, loadv(src + k * N + 4));\n        }\n    }\n    const size_t rest = jLen - fullPanels * NR;\n    if (rest > 0) {\n        double* dst = packed + fullPanels * kLen * NR;\n        const double* src = B + kBegin * N + jBegin + fullPanels * NR;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kLen; ++k) {\n            size_t c = 0;\n            for (; c < rest; ++c) dst[k * NR + c] = src[k * N + c];\n            for (; c < NR; ++c) dst[k * NR + c] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Update an R x NR tile of C (R rows starting at C, stride N) with the packed\n// panel for kLen consecutive k values. A points at row 0, column kBegin.\ntemplate <size_t R>\nstatic inline void microKernel(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ Bp,\n                               double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N, const size_t kLen) {\n    v4d acc0[R], acc1[R];\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n        acc0[r] = loadv(C + r * N);\n        acc1[r] = loadv(C + r * N + 4);\n    }\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < kLen; ++k) {\n        const v4d b0 = loadv(Bp + k * NR);\n        const v4d b1 = loadv(Bp + k * NR + 4);\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n            const v4d a = bcast(A[r * N + k]);\n            acc0[r] += a * b0;\n            acc1[r] += a * b1;\n        }\n    }\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n        storev(C + r * N, acc0[r]);\n        storev(C + r * N + 4, acc1[r]);\n    }\n}\n\n// Same as microKernel but for a partial (ncols < NR) last panel: the panel is\n// zero-padded, so compute on a full-width temporary and copy back ncols values.\ntemplate <size_t R>\nstatic inline void microKernelEdge(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ Bp,\n                                   double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N, const size_t kLen,\n                                   const size_t ncols) {\n    alignas(32) double tmp[R][NR];\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r)\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < NR; ++c) tmp[r][c] = c < ncols ? C[r * N + c] : 0.0;\n    microKernel<R>(A, Bp, &tmp[0][0], NR, kLen);\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r)\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < ncols; ++c) C[r * N + c] = tmp[r][c];\n}\n\n// Apply all panels of a packed block to R rows of C.\ntemplate <size_t R>\nstatic inline void rowPanel(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ Bp,\n                            double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N, const size_t jLen,\n                            const size_t kLen) {\n    const size_t fullPanels = jLen / NR;\n    for (size_t p = 0; p < fullPanels; ++p)\n        microKernel<R>(A, Bp + p * kLen * NR, C + p * NR, N, kLen);\n    const size_t rest = jLen - fullPanels * NR;\n    if (rest > 0)\n        microKernelEdge<R>(A, Bp + fullPanels * kLen * NR, C + fullPanels * NR, N, kLen, rest);\n}\n\nvoid matrixMultiplyRows(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                        std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N, const size_t numRows) {\n    std::fill(C.begin(), C.begin() + numRows * N, 0.0);\n    if (numRows == 0) return;\n\n    const double* a = A.data();\n    const double* b = B.data();\n    double* c = C.data();\n\n    // Packed B block buffer: BK x (BJ rounded up to a multiple of NR).\n    std::vector<double> packed(BK * ((std::min(BJ, N) + NR - 1) / NR) * NR);\n\n    for (size_t jj = 0; jj < N; jj += BJ) {\n        const size_t jEnd = std::min(N, jj + BJ);\n        const size_t jLen = jEnd - jj;\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < N; kk += BK) {\n            const size_t kEnd = std::min(N, kk + BK);\n            const size_t kLen = kEnd - kk;\n            packB(b, packed.data(), N, kk, kEnd, jj, jEnd);\n\n            size_t i = 0;\n            for (; i + MR <= numRows; i += MR)\n                rowPanel<MR>(a + i * N + kk, packed.data(), c + i * N + jj, N, jLen, kLen);\n            switch (numRows - i) {\n                case 3: rowPanel<3>(a + i * N + kk, packed.data(), c + i * N + jj, N, jLen, kLen); break;\n                case 2: rowPanel<2>(a + i * N + kk, packed.data(), c + i * N + jj, N, jLen, kLen); break;\n                case 1: rowPanel<1>(a + i * N + kk, packed.data(), c + i * N + jj, N, jLen, kLen); break;\n                default: break;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:start]+new+s[end:]\nopen('k4.cpp','w').write(s)\nEOF\nrun(){ sed -e \"s/constexpr size_t BK = 256;/constexpr size_t BK = $1;/\" -e \"s/constexpr size_t BJ = 512;/constexpr size_t BJ = $2;/\" k4.cpp > kv4.cpp; mpicxx -O3 -march=native -ffp-contract=off -std=c++20 -Wall -Wextra kv4.cpp -o kv4 2>&1 | grep -E \"error|warning\" ; echo \"BK=$1 BJ=$2: $(mpirun -np 1 ./kv4 -n 2048 | grep GFLOPS)\"; }; run 256 512; run 128 512; run 256 1024; run 512 512; run 256 2048; run 128 128; echo \"hash check:\"; for n in 512 1000 7 517; do echo \"$n: $(./mm_orig/orig $n | grep Hash) | $(mpirun -np 3 ./kv4 -n $n -r -v | grep -E 'Hash|Validation:' | tr '\\n' ' ')\"; The benchmark in `matmul/` now runs unconditionally under MPI, with the same executable name, the same two files, and no new dependencies beyond MPI itself.\n\n**Parallelization approach**\n\n- 1D row decomposition. Each rank owns a contiguous block of rows of A and C. Uneven N is handled by giving the first N mod P ranks one extra row.\n- No input communication. The matrix generator is deterministic, so every rank initializes its own A rows and a full local copy of B directly instead of broadcasting.\n- C is gathered to rank 0 only when `-r` or `-v` is given, using a row-sized derived datatype so counts stay in rows and cannot overflow int for large N.\n- Timing is a barrier followed by max-reduced MPI wall time, reported in ms as before. All printing, result output, and validation happen on rank 0. The exit code is broadcast so every rank returns the same status.\n\n**Local kernel** (where the single-rank speedup comes from)\n\n- Cache-blocked over column and k blocks. Each B block is packed once into contiguous 8-column panels, which removes the L1 set aliasing that row strides of N*8 bytes cause for power-of-two N.\n- A 4x8 tile of C is held in vector registers over a whole k block using GCC vector extensions, so there are no stack spills in the hot loop.\n- For every C element the k-sum is still accumulated strictly in increasing k, and `-ffp-contract=off` is set in CMakeLists so multiply and add round separately like the original's compiled loop. Results are bit-identical to the original for even N. For odd N the original binary uses a fused multiply-add on the last k element only (a GCC code-generation quirk), so hashes differ by one ulp there while validation passes.\n\n**Verification** on this 64-core EPYC node (cores run at about 2 GHz):\n\n| Ranks | N=4096 time | GFLOPS |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | 10119 ms | 13.6 |\n| 8 | 1328 ms | 103 |\n| 32 | 399 ms | 344 |\n| 64 | 191 ms | 720 |\n\nHashes and validation were checked against the original kernel for N in {1, 2, 7, 9, 100, 512, 517, 1000} with 1, 3, 5 and 8 ranks. The original single-threaded code takes about 100 s at N=2048 versus 1.3 s for one MPI rank, so the serial baseline also improved substantially.\n\n**CMake changes**: `find_package(MPI REQUIRED)`, link `MPI::MPI_CXX`, and the added `-ffp-contract=off` flag."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":401,"cache_read_input_tokens":66912,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":401},"output_tokens":750,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"efce3ce7-e04c-4e41-bb00-1c0c7304f0ed","uuid":"60d14e3e-1a56-4d37-83f1-69618c0b4525","timestamp":"2026-11-07T07:11:05.068Z","request_id":"req_011Cgo9fmiahfpijbTaXv5rT"}
